       41 +# ------------------------------------------------------------
          +- spike trains

       44  @dataclass
       45 +class SpikeTrains:                                            
       46 +    """Ragged spike times (ms), CSR-style: `times[offsets[i]:o
          +ffsets[i+1]]`."""                                             
       47 +    times: np.ndarray            # float32, concatenated      
       48 +    offsets: np.ndarray          # int64, (n + 1,)            
       49 +    overflow: np.ndarray         # bool, (n,)                 
       50 +                                                              
       51 +    @property                                                 
       52 +    def n(self) -> int:                                       
       53 +        return self.offsets.size - 1                          
       54 +                                                              
       55 +    @property                                                 
       56 +    def counts(self) -> np.ndarray:                           
       57 +        return np.diff(self.offsets)                          
       58 +                                                              
       59 +    def get(self, i: int) -> np.ndarray:                      
       60 +        return self.times[self.offsets[i]:self.offsets[i + 1]]
       61 +                                                              
       62 +    def subset(self, idx: Sequence[int]) -> "SpikeTrains":    
       63 +        idx = np.asarray(idx, dtype=np.int64)                 
       64 +        parts = [self.times[self.offsets[i]:self.offsets[i + 1
          +]] for i in idx]                                              
       65 +        counts = np.array([p.size for p in parts], dtype=np.in
          +t64)                                                          
       66 +        off = np.zeros(idx.size + 1, dtype=np.int64)          
       67 +        np.cumsum(counts, out=off[1:])                        
       68 +        times = (np.concatenate(parts) if parts else          
       69 +                 np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float32))               
       70 +        return SpikeTrains(times.astype(np.float32, copy=False
          +), off,                                                       
       71 +                           self.overflow[idx].copy())         
       72 +                                                              
       73 +    @staticmethod                                             
       74 +    def concat(parts: Sequence["SpikeTrains"]) -> "SpikeTrains
          +":                                                            
       75 +        parts = [p for p in parts if p is not None]           
       76 +        if not parts:                                         
       77 +            return SpikeTrains(np.zeros(0, np.float32), np.zer
          +os(1, np.int64),                                              
       78 +                               np.zeros(0, bool))             
       79 +        times = np.concatenate([p.times for p in parts])      
       80 +        counts = np.concatenate([p.counts for p in parts])    
       81 +        off = np.zeros(counts.size + 1, dtype=np.int64)       
       82 +        np.cumsum(counts, out=off[1:])                        
       83 +        return SpikeTrains(times, off,                        
       84 +                           np.concatenate([p.overflow for p in
          + parts]))                                                     
       85 +                                                              
       86 +    @staticmethod                                             
       87 +    def from_dense(dense: np.ndarray, counts: np.ndarray,     
       88 +                   overflow: np.ndarray) -> "SpikeTrains":    
       89 +        """`dense` is (max_spikes, n) as written by the kernel
          +."""                                                          
       90 +        counts = counts.astype(np.int64)                      
       91 +        off = np.zeros(counts.size + 1, dtype=np.int64)       
       92 +        np.cumsum(counts, out=off[1:])                        
       93 +        times = np.empty(int(off[-1]), dtype=np.float32)      
       94 +        for i in range(counts.size):                          
       95 +            times[off[i]:off[i + 1]] = dense[:counts[i], i]   
       96 +        return SpikeTrains(times, off, overflow.astype(bool)) 
       97 +                                                              
       98 +    def replace_rows(self, rows: np.ndarray, other: "SpikeTrai
          +ns") -> "SpikeTrains":                                        
       99 +        """Return a copy with `rows` taken from `other` (row k
          + <- other row k)."""                                          
      100 +        rows = np.asarray(rows, dtype=np.int64)               
      101 +        parts: List[np.ndarray] = []                          
      102 +        counts = self.counts.copy()                           
      103 +        pos = {int(r): k for k, r in enumerate(rows)}

      111 +        np.cumsum(counts, out=off[1:])                        
      112 +        ov = self.overflow.copy()                             
      113 +        ov[rows] = other.overflow                             
      114 +        return SpikeTrains(np.concatenate(parts).astype(np.flo

          + nameability merge

       97 +def nameability_merge(labels: np.ndarray, desc: Dict[str, np.n
          +darray],                                                      
       98 +                      cfg: Config) -> Tuple[np.ndarray, List[D
          +ict], Dict]:                                                  
       99 +    """Merge clusters whose descriptor distributions are indis
          +tinguishable.                                                 
      100 +                                                              
      101 +    Pairwise two-sided Mann-Whitney U on every descriptor in  
      102 +    `MERGE_DESCRIPTORS`; a pair is indistinguishable when *eve
          +ry* descriptor's                                              
      103 +    p-value exceeds the Bonferroni-corrected threshold        
      104 +    `alpha / (n_pairs * n_descriptors)`.  Indistinguishable pa
          +irs are merged                                                
      105 +    transitively (union-find), so a chain A~B~C becomes one cl
          +uster.                                                        
      106 +                                                              
      107 +    Returns `(merged_labels, pair_rows, summary)`; `merged_lab
          +els` keeps the                                                
      108 +    smallest original id of each merged group.                
      109 +    """                                                       
      110 +    from scipy.stats import mannwhitneyu                      
      111 +                                                              
      112 +    ids = np.unique(labels[labels >= 0])                      
      113 +    k = ids.size                                              
      114 +    pairs = list(itertools.combinations(range(k), 2))         
      115 +    n_desc = len(MERGE_DESCRIPTORS)                           
      116 +    if k < 2:                                                 
      117 +        return labels.copy(), [], {"n_before": int(k), "n_afte
          +r": int(k),                                                   
      118 +                                   "alpha": float("nan"), "n_p
          +airs": 0,                                                     
      119 +                                   "n_merged_pairs": 0}       
      120 +    alpha = cfg.gates.merge_alpha / max(len(pairs) * n_desc, 1
          +)                                                             
      121 +                                                              
      122 +    cols = {d: [desc[d][labels == cid] for cid in ids] for d i
          +n MERGE_DESCRIPTORS}                                          
      123 +    parent = list(range(k))                                   
      124 +                                                              
      125 +    def find(i):                                              
      126 +        while parent[i] != i:                                 
      127 +            parent[i] = parent[parent[i]]                     
      128 +            i = parent[i]                                     
      129 +        return i                                              
      130 +                                                              
      131 +    def union(i, j):                                          
      132 +        ri, rj = find(i), find(j)                             
      133 +        if ri != rj:                                          
      134 +            parent[max(ri, rj)] = min(ri, rj)                 
      135 +                                                              
      136 +    rows: List[Dict] = []                                     
      137 +    for i, j in pairs:                                        
      138 +        ps = {}                                               
      139 +        for d in MERGE_DESCRIPTORS:                           
      140 +            a = cols[d][i][np.isfinite(cols[d][i])]           
      141 +            b = cols[d][j][np.isfinite(cols[d][j])]           
      142 +            if a.size < 3 or b.size < 3:                      
      143 +                ps[d] = 1.0                                   
      144 +                continue                                      
      145 +            if np.array_equal(np.unique(a), np.unique(b)) and 
          +np.unique(a).size == 1:                                       
      146 +                ps[d] = 1.0                                   
      147 +                continue                                      
      148 +            try:                                              
      149 +                ps[d] = float(mannwhitneyu(a, b, alternative="
          +two-sided").pvalue)                                           
      150 +            except ValueError:                                
      151 +                ps[d] = 1.0                                   
      152 +        same = all(p > alpha for p in ps.values())            
      153 +        if same:                                              
      154 +            union(i, j)                                       
      155 +            rows.append({"a": int(ids[i]), "b": int(ids[j]),  
      156 +                         "min_p": float(min(ps.values())),    
      157 +                         **{f"p_{d}": ps[d] for d in MERGE_DES
          +CRIPTORS}})                                                   
      158 +                                                              
      159 +    merged = labels.copy()                                    
      160 +    groups: Dict[int, List[int]] = {}                         
      161 +    for i in range(k):                                        
      162 +        groups.setdefault(find(i), []).append(i)              
      163 +    for root, members in groups.items():                      
      164 +        if len(members) == 1:                                 
      165 +            continue                                          
      166 +        keep = int(ids[min(members)])                         
      167 +        for m in members:                                     
      168 +            merged[labels == ids[m]] = keep                   
      169 +                                                              
      170 +    n_after = int(np.unique(merged[merged >= 0]).size)        
      171 +    summary = {"n_before": int(k), "n_after": n_after, "alpha"
          +: float(alpha),                                               
      172 +               "n_pairs": len(pairs), "n_merged_pairs": len(ro
          +ws),                                                          
      173 +               "groups": [[int(ids[m]) for m in g] for g in gr
          +oups.values()                                                 
      174 +                          if len(g) > 1]}                     
      175 +    return merged, rows, summary

       86 +def read_spike_times_csv(path: str, n_rows: Optional[int] = No
          +ne):

          +Trains)."""

          +p.float32))

       99 +                            np.zeros(counts.size, dtype=bool))

      838 +def _dt_halved_features(cfg: Config, sim: Simulator, S: Dict, 
          +scales, weights,                                              
      839 +                        pca, active_idx: np.ndarray, rng, log,
          + timer):                                                      
      840 +    """Re-simulate a subsample at `dt_int/2` and featurise it 
          +(§8.2)."""                                                    
      841 +    m = int(min(cfg.clustering.stability_subsample, active_idx
          +.size))                                                       
      842 +    rows = np.sort(rng.choice(active_idx, size=m, replace=Fals
          +e))                                                           
      843 +    g = grid_for_dt(cfg.grid, cfg.grid.dt_int / 2.0)          
      844 +    parts: List[SpikeTrains] = []                             
      845 +    with timer.section("simulate_dt_half"):                   
      846 +        for s0 in range(0, rows.size, cfg.schedule.chunk):    
      847 +            sl = rows[s0:s0 + cfg.schedule.chunk]             
      848 +            res = sim.run(S["p"][sl, :4], S["drive"][sl], grid
          +=g,                                                           
      849 +                          store_traces=False, max_spikes=cfg.i
          +si.s_max)                                                     
      850 +            parts.append(res.spikes)                          
      851 +            del res                                           
      852 +    sp = SpikeTrains.concat(parts)                            
      853 +    st = isim.isi_set(sp, cfg.grid.t_transient, cfg.grid.t_ste
          +ady_end)                                                      
      854 +    on = isim.isi_set(sp, 0.0, cfg.grid.t_transient)          
      855 +    bs = isim.compute_blocks(st, cfg, h=weights.h)            
      856 +    bo = isim.compute_blocks(on, cfg, h=weights.h)            
      857 +    dsc = isim.descriptors(st, cfg.grid.window_seconds, cfg)  
      858 +    ex = isim.onset_extras(on, sp, cfg, dsc["mean_isi"])      
      859 +    F = isim._l2_rows(np.hstack([                             
      860 +        isim.steady_vector(bs, scales, weights),              
      861 +        weights.w_onset * isim.onset_vector(bo, ex, scales, we
          +ights)]))                                                     
      862 +    log(f"  dt/2 re-simulation: {rows.size} samples at dt_int=
          +"                                                             
      863 +        f"{g.dt_int:g} ms for the §8.2 step-size test")       
      864 +    return pca.transform(F).astype(np.float32), rows          
      865  
      866  
      867 +def _fine_spectra(cfg: Config, sim: Simulator, params: np.ndar
          +ray, drive):                                                  
      868 +    """§3.5: re-simulate at `dt_out = dt_int` and rFFT host-si
          +de."""                                                        
      869 +    g = replace(cfg.grid, L=1)                                
      870 +    n0 = int(round(cfg.grid.t_transient / g.dt_out)) - 1      
      871 +    n_out = int(round((cfg.grid.t_steady_end - cfg.grid.t_tran
          +sient) / g.dt_out))                                           
      872 +    res = sim.run(params, drive, grid=g, store_traces=True, re
          +fine=True,                                                    
      873 +                  out_start=n0, n_out=n_out, peak_hold=False) 
      874 +    freqs, mag = features.rfft_magnitude(res.traces, g.dt_out)
      875 +    del res                                                   
      876 +    return freqs, mag                                         
      877 +                                                              
      878 +                                                              
      879 +def _subthreshold_report(cfg: Config, sim: Simulator, S: Dict,
          + cls, log,                                                    
      880 +                         timer) -> List[Dict]:                
      881 +    """§3.4: the `S` class is reported with its resonant peak 
          +frequency."""                                                 
      882 +    idx = np.where(cls.subthreshold)[0]                       
      883 +    if idx.size == 0:                                         
      884 +        log("  S class (subthreshold/resonant): none")        
      885 +        return []                                             
      886 +    cap = int(min(idx.size, 512))                             
      887 +    sel = idx[:cap]                                           
      888 +    with timer.section("subthreshold"):                       
      889 +        freqs, mag = _fine_spectra(cfg, sim, S["p"][sel], S["d
          +rive"][sel])                                                  
      890 +        pk = features.peak_frequency(freqs, mag)              
      891 +    rows = [{"sample_id": int(i), "a": float(S["p"][i, 0]),   
      892 +             "b": float(S["p"][i, 1]), "c": float(S["p"][i, 2]
          +),                                                            
      893 +             "d": float(S["p"][i, 3]), "delta": float(S["p"][i
          +, 4]),                                                        
      894 +             "I": float(S["drive"][i]), "sd_v": float(S["diag"
          +]["sd_v"][i]),                                                
      895 +             "resonant_peak_hz": float(p)} for i, p in zip(sel
          +, pk)]                                                        
      896 +    log(f"  S class (subthreshold/resonant): {idx.size} sample
          +s, "                                                          
      897 +        f"median resonant peak {np.median(pk):.1f} Hz")       
      898 +    return rows

       1  # Izhikevich spiking-neuron cluster discovery
       2

       4 -`(a, b, c, d)` of the Izhikevich spiking-neuron model at fixed

         +for

       7 -The design is specified in [`SPEC.md`](SPEC.md) — read §1 for t
         -he objective and

         -easurement                                                     
       9 -forced a change to the spec. `spikes.pdf` is the reference pape

       7 +The v1 design is specified in [`SPEC.md`](SPEC.md); **[`IMPROVE
         +MENTS.md`](IMPROVEMENTS.md)                                    
       8 +specifies v2 and is the document the code implements** — read i
         +ts §1 for the

      12  
      13 +What changed in v2, in one line each:                          
      14 +                                                               
      15 +| | change |                                                   
      16 +|---|---|                                                      
      17 +| **C1** | the clustering input is an **encoding of the ISI seq
         +uence** (a return-map KDE, not a 128-band magnitude spectrum) |
      18 +| **C2** | spike times are captured **in-kernel at `dt_int` res
         +olution** |                                                    
      19 +| **C3** | the drive is `I = I*(θ) + Δ` — a controlled overdriv
         +e above each sample's **numerically determined rheobase**, not 
         +a fixed `I = 10` |                                             
      20 +| **C4** | the convergence gate is judged on **spike times**, n
         +ot on the feature metric |                                     
      21 +| **C5** | the **onset transient** is featurised rather than di
         +scarded |                                                      
      22 +| **C6** | GP active learning is retired for a single **Sobol**
         + design at 2.5× the sample count |                             
      23 +| **C7/C8** | **representation-health gates**, stability tests 
         +that can fail, a **nameability merge**, acceptance criteria tha
         +t can fail |                                                   
      24 +| **C9** | the batched spectral path is retired; the rFFT is ho
         +st-side and on demand |                                        
      25 +

● Short answer to your question while the rerun finishes: yes — the §5.1 
  amendment changed three things in the implementation, all now in place.

  1. convergence_gate now evaluates the three checks in fractional form (≥ 90%
  of used probes) with the bounded worst cases you specified — it tracks
  per-probe |Δn| and the worst per-ISI error, which the strict rule did not
  need.
  2. Criterion 2's per-ISI statistic is computed over the common prefix when a
  probe's counts differ, so a probe that criterion 1's one-spike tolerance
  admits is still measurable under criterion 2 (otherwise it was NaN and
  consumed tolerance twice).
  3. The selected step moved from dt_int = 0.0025 ms to 0.01 ms, exactly as your
  §5.1 table predicts (0.02 ms still fails at 74% count agreement), which cuts
  the production integration 4×. V2-A15 in §13.1 has been rewritten to record
  that §5.1 supersedes the strict reading.

      29      return np.array([letters.get(int(l), "noise") for l in labe

      30  
      31  
      32 +def _spread(anchors: Sequence, fontsize: float, iters: int = 40
         +0):                                                            
      33 +    """Push overlapping label positions apart, in data coordina
         +tes.                                                           
      34 +                                                               
      35 +    A plain repulsion between label centres: each pair closer t
         +han the                                                        
      36 +    estimated label size is separated a little, every pass, wit
         +h a weak spring                                                
      37 +    back to the anchor so labels stay near their cluster.      
      38 +    """                                                        
      39 +    P = np.asarray(anchors, dtype=np.float64)                  
      40 +    if P.shape[0] < 2:                                         
      41 +        return P                                               
      42 +    span = max(P[:, 0].ptp(), P[:, 1].ptp(), 1e-9)             
      43 +    r = span * (0.012 + 0.0016 * fontsize)        # ~ one label
         + box                                                           
      44 +    pos = P.copy()                                             
      45 +    for _ in range(iters):                                     
      46 +        d = pos[:, None, :] - pos[None, :, :]                  
      47 +        dist = np.hypot(d[..., 0], d[..., 1])                  
      48 +        np.fill_diagonal(dist, np.inf)                         
      49 +        close = dist < r                                       
      50 +        if not close.any():                                    
      51 +            break                                              
      52 +        push = np.zeros_like(pos)                              
      53 +        ii, jj = np.where(close)                               
      54 +        v = d[ii, jj]                                          
      55 +        n = np.maximum(dist[ii, jj], 1e-9)[:, None]            
      56 +        step = v / n * ((r - dist[ii, jj])[:, None] * 0.5)     
      57 +        np.add.at(push, ii, step)                              
      58 +        pos += 0.5 * push                                      
      59 +        pos += 0.06 * (P - pos)                   # spring back
         + to the anchor                                                 
      60 +    return pos                                                 
      61 +                                                               
      62 +                                                               
      63  def scatter_2d(path: str, E2: np.ndarray, labels: np.ndarray,
      64                 letters: Dict[int, str], quarantine: np.ndarray,
      65                 placement_xy: Sequence, flags: Dict[str, Dict[in
          t, bool]],

  What changed

  where: new src/izhiclust/isi.py

  Tests: 48 pass (new test_isi.py, test_rheobase.py, test_spike_capture.py,
  test_merge.py, test_gate.py).

  The rerun — N = 65 536, 38.1 min

  The §8.1 gates, which are the hypothesis, pass decisively: PCA components for
  99% variance 13 of 372 (v1: 92 of 128); near/far distance ratio 0.015 (v1
  0.818, saturated); octave pairs 0.096 vs 1.5× pairs 0.040 (v1 inverted at
  0.379 vs 0.501). Zero-spike samples 0 (v1: 45.1%), 97.6% of samples clustered.
  CH, which v1 named slow tonic, now lands at p = 1.000 in a bursting cluster
  whose burst-index IQR contains its own 0.604. Round-trip from spike_times.csv
  is exact.

  Four §9 criteria fail, and they are one finding: the partition is not
  reproducible at the resolution DBCV selects — seed ARI 0.559, bootstrap 0.249,
  window 0.501, dt 0.439; only 5 of 56 clusters pass both the stability and
  contiguity gates. v1's 0.780 is not comparable: it was measured with
  min_cluster_size unscaled on a subsample, i.e. against a coarser taxonomy than
  it reported (that was a real bug, fixed — see V2-A31). Written up in §14.2:
  C1 and C3 worked; the clustering stage is where v2 is weak.

  Two deviations you should look at

  - V2-A7/A9 — §3.3's "every block L2-normalised per sample" is applied to A and
  B only. Block D is one scalar (normalising it leaves only its sign) and block
  C is exactly zero for a regular train; both are scaled dataset-wide instead,
  with floors. Without this the rate axis disappears and block C amplifies
  roundoff.
  - V2-A12 — gate criterion 2 measures per-ISI error, not absolute spike-time
  error, which accumulates with spike index (1.10 → 0.067 ms as dt goes 0.04 →
  0.0025) and so measures window length. Your §5.1 adopted this.

  I left the working tree uncommitted. One caveat on the artefacts:
  output/clusters_umap2d.png was redrawn after the run from data/run_data.npz
  through the same (now label-separating) plots.scatter_2d, because 56 labels
  overprinted each other; everything else in output/ is as the run wrote it.
